We’ve made it simpler for fleet managers with our EV Hub.</p></div> </a></div></div> <a href="https://www.fleetalliance.co.uk/insights" class="button button__with--border mt70">View All</a><div class="home-content-block mt30" style="text-align: initial;"><div class="container"> &nbsp;<h3 style="text-align: center">Fleet Management with Fleet Alliance</h3><p style="text-align: center">Fleet Alliance is one of the leading independent Fleet Management specialists in the UK. From SME to large corporates, we help companies of all sizes manage their vehicle fleet while reducing operating costs and delivering the highest levels of service in our industry.</p><p style="text-align: center">Our award-winning customer service is the result of continual product innovation and staff engagement. We are four-time winners of the leasing industry’s benchmark customer service survey, Fleeteye, and have been named one of the UK’s Best Workplaces 13 years in a row.</p><p style="text-align: center">Our growth is underpinned by strong business, community and environmental ethics. We have raised almost £1 million for local communities and charities. We're a certified carbon-neutral company, and as a member of the Climate Group EV100 global initiative, we are one of a group of forward-thinking companies committed to accelerating the transition to electric vehicles and making sustainable mobility the new normal.</p> &nbsp;<p style="text-align: center"><a class="button button__primary--red" href="https://www.fleetalliance.co.uk/fleet-management-services/">Get started</a></p> &nbsp;  &nbsp;  &nbsp;<h3 class="case-reset semibold wide-margin-bottom" style="text-align: center">Common questions and answers about fleet management</h3> &nbsp;<div class="fleet-accordions" data-fleet-accordion-group><div class="fleet-accordion fleet-accordion--open" data-fleet-accordion-toggle-class="fleet-accordion--open" data-fleet-accordion><header class="fleet-accordion__header" data-fleet-accordion-header> What is fleet management? <i class="fa fa-minus fleet-accordion__icon fleet-accordion__icon--close" data-fleet-accordion-icon-close></i> <i class="fa fa-plus fleet-accordion__icon fleet-accordion__icon--open" data-fleet-accordion-icon-open></i></header><div class="fleet-accordion__content" data-fleet-accordion-content><p>Fleet management is the process of managing an organisation&#8217;s commercial vehicles, ensuring the optimal performance of those vehicles, and ensuring the fleet operation is efficient and cost-effective. This can include but is not limited to leasing and funding of vehicles, driver management, fuel management, accident management, and fleet risk assessments.</p></div></div><div class="fleet-accordion" data-fleet-accordion-toggle-class="fleet-accordion--open" data-fleet-accordion><header class="fleet-accordion__header" data-fleet-accordion-header> What does a fleet management company do? <i class="fa fa-minus fleet-accordion__icon fleet-accordion__icon--close" data-fleet-accordion-icon-close></i> <i class="fa fa-plus fleet-accordion__icon fleet-accordion__icon--open" data-fleet-accordion-icon-open></i></header><div class="fleet-accordion__content" data-fleet-accordion-content style="display: none;"><p>A fleet management company helps companies streamline the running of their vehicles through the provision of consultancy and technology which can provide real-time fleet intelligence, improve fleet efficiency, and ultimately, reduce fleet costs. Fleet Alliance guarantees the cheapest lease rentals available on the market for every single vehicle we provide through our unique competitive tendering model.</p></div></div><div class="fleet-accordion" data-fleet-accordion-toggle-class="fleet-accordion--open" data-fleet-accordion><header class="fleet-accordion__header" data-fleet-accordion-header> Why is fleet management important? <i class="fa fa-minus fleet-accordion__icon fleet-accordion__icon--close" data-fleet-accordion-icon-close></i> <i class="fa fa-plus fleet-accordion__icon fleet-accordion__icon--open" data-fleet-accordion-icon-open></i></header><div class="fleet-accordion__content" data-fleet-accordion-content style="display: none;"><p>Fleet management is typically one of the largest company costs. However, when managed effectively fleet management can significantly reduce the financial burden of running a fleet of vehicles, whilst ensuring maximum efficiency and a reduction in administration. In essence, fleet management provides companies with greater control of their fleet for less effort, whilst reducing overall costs. That&#8217;s why it&#8217;s a win-win for our clients.</p></div></div><div class="fleet-accordion" data-fleet-accordion-toggle-class="fleet-accordion--open" data-fleet-accordion><header class="fleet-accordion__header" data-fleet-accordion-header> Who uses fleet management? <i class="fa fa-minus fleet-accordion__icon fleet-accordion__icon--close" data-fleet-accordion-icon-close></i> <i class="fa fa-plus fleet-accordion__icon fleet-accordion__icon--open" data-fleet-accordion-icon-open></i></header><div class="fleet-accordion__content" data-fleet-accordion-content style="display: none;"><p>Fleet management services are used by companies of all sizes across all sectors. This includes construction, oil and gas, engineering, security, energy, pharmaceutical, and even non-profit industries. You can view a sample of <a href="https://www.fleetalliance.co.uk/case-studies/">company testimonials here</a>.</p></div></div><div class="fleet-accordion" data-fleet-accordion-toggle-class="fleet-accordion--open" data-fleet-accordion><header class="fleet-accordion__header" data-fleet-accordion-header> How do fleet management companies work? <i class="fa fa-minus fleet-accordion__icon fleet-accordion__icon--close" data-fleet-accordion-icon-close></i> <i class="fa fa-plus fleet-accordion__icon fleet-accordion__icon--open" data-fleet-accordion-icon-open></i></header><div class="fleet-accordion__content" data-fleet-accordion-content style="display: none;"><p>Fleet management companies essentially remove the cost and hassle of managing your fleet leaving you and your team free to look after your customers and grow your business.
